function displayWindow(windowname,reason) --reason is optional and only for the errorwarning
	enabled.Value = false
	local window = frame:FindFirstChild(windowname)
	if window then
		if reason ~= nil then
			window.Reason.Text = reason
		end
		window.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until window.Visible == false
	end
	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

		local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WearOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend)
		if request == true then
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit worn successfully!")
		elseif request == false then
			if statuses ~= nil then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
						v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
					end
				end
			end
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
		else
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","Please wait a few more moments before trying this again.")
		end
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function SetBarOverheadText()
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysCount").Text = stats.Spins.Value == 0 and "Visit 5 days in a row = free RHS prize spins!" or stats.Spins.Value > 0 and "Congrats! You earned "..stats.Spins.Value.." free prize spin"..(stats.Spins.Value == 1 and "" or "s").."!"
end

function TextTransparencyFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local transincrement = (1 - element.TextTransparency) / totalframes
		local strokeincrement = (1 - element.TextStrokeTransparency) / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.TextTransparency = element.TextTransparency + transincrement * mult
				element.TextStrokeTransparency = element.TextStrokeTransparency + strokeincrement * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - (transincrement * totalframes)
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - (strokeincrement * totalframes)
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function ImageFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local increment = 1 / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
		else
			element.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.ImageTransparency = element.ImageTransparency + increment * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function PlaySound(id,timelength,volume,pitch)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.Name = "LoyaltySound"
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..id
	sound.Volume = volume or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound:Play()
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,timelength or 3)
end

				PlaySound(131886985)
			end

			SetBarOverheadText()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = true
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = true

		else

		end

	frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = true
	frame.OpenSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.RHSLPLabel, 20, false)
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.TotalLP, 20, false)
		frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = false
		PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
		frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,240), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -125),nil,nil, .4,true)
		wait(.32)
		
		local spinning = false
		local cyclerate = 12
		local winningindex
		
		local function CycleSpinner()
			local recentlyusedtable = {}
			local function DupeCheck(i)
				for _,v in pairs(recentlyusedtable) do
					if v == i then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			while spinning do
				local indextodisplay
				local winner = false
				if winningindex ~= nil and DupeCheck(winningindex) == false then
					indextodisplay = winningindex
					winner = true
				else
					repeat
						local n = math.random(1,#prizetable)
						if DupeCheck(n) == false then
							indextodisplay = n
						end
					until indextodisplay ~= nil
				end
				table.insert(recentlyusedtable,indextodisplay)
				if #recentlyusedtable > 5 then
					table.remove(recentlyusedtable,1)
				end
				
				ShowSpinPrize(prizetable,indextodisplay,winner)
				
				if winner then
					spinning = false
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
						frame.RespinButton.Visible = true
					end
					return
				end
				
				wait(1/cyclerate)
			end
		end
		
		local function RunSpin()
			winningindex = nil
			frame.RespinButton.Visible = false
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = false
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
			cyclerate = 12
			prizetable = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable:InvokeServer()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Visible = true
			frame.StopSpinner.Visible = true
			spinning = true
			spawn(CycleSpinner)
			
			local stopcon
			stopcon = frame.StopSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				stopcon:disconnect()
				frame.StopSpinner.Visible = false
				PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
				wait(math.random(3,6)/10)
				for i=1,10 do
					wait(.6 + math.random(1,10)/100 - cyclerate / 40)
					cyclerate = cyclerate - 1
				end
				wait(math.random(1,25)/10)
				winningindex = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize:InvokeServer()
				local respincon
				respincon = frame.RespinButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 and spinning == false then
						respincon:disconnect()
						PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
						RunSpin()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		
		RunSpin()
	end)
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

function removeMarker()
	if connection then
		connection:disconnect()
		connection = nil
	end
	if marker then
		marker:Destroy()
		marker = nil
	end
end

function createMarker()
	marker = Instance.new("Part")
	marker.Name = "Marker"
	marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Lime green")
	marker.TopSurface = "Smooth"
	marker.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
	marker.FormFactor = "Custom"
	marker.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
	marker.Anchored = true
	marker.CanCollide = false
	
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
	mesh.MeshType = "Sphere"
	mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(25,7,25)
	mesh.Offset = Vector3.new(0,-2,0)
	mesh.Parent = marker
	
	marker.Parent = Tool
end

script.Parent.Activated:connect(function()
	if enabled then
		enabled = false
		if mouse.Target and mouse.Target.Name == "Road" then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnCarRequest:InvokeServer(mouse.Hit)
			if request == true then
				if plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpawnCarGui") then
					plr.PlayerGui.SpawnCarGui:Destroy()
				end
				removeMarker()
				Tool:Destroy()
			end
		end
		wait(.15)
		enabled = true
	end
end)

script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function()
	local clonegui = script.SpawnCarGui:clone()
	clonegui.Parent = plr.PlayerGui
	clonegui.Frame.Visible = true
	
	connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
		if not marker then createMarker() end
		
		if mouse and mouse.Hit and mouse.Target then
			marker.CFrame = CFrame.new(mouse.Hit.p) + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
			if (torso.Position - mouse.Hit.p).magnitude > 52 or mouse.Target.Name ~= "Road" then
				marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Really red")
			else
				marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Lime green")
			end
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()
	if plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpawnCarGui") then
		plr.PlayerGui.SpawnCarGui:Destroy()
	end
	removeMarker()
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local vol = plr:WaitForChild("VolumeSetting")
local clubvol = plr:WaitForChild("ClubVolumeSetting")
local boomboxsetting = plr:WaitForChild("BoomboxSetting")
local day = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Day")

local songholder = game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems

local songs = {} --the sets in the order that they should play based on the rng

local rng = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RNG").Value
local weekdayspassed = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("WeekdaysPassed")
local musicsets = { --4 tables of each music set (1-3 weekdays, 4 weekends) (overnight to evening)
	{503064767,503065215,503066771,503063705},
	{503064767,503064607,503066799,503063839},
	{503064767,503065423,503066871,503063867},
	{503065984,503065630,503066928,503064485},
}

local neg = 1
if rng % 10 <= 4 then
	neg = -1
end
for i=1,3 do
	songs[i] = musicsets[(rng + i * neg) % 3 + 1]
end
songs[4] = musicsets[4]


local fading = false
local location
--Outside, Club Red, Madi's Music

local avg_loudness = 0
local highest_loudness = 0

local shifting = false --whether the volume is currently being shifted

local defaultid = "rbxassetid://143080540" --the default club music

local current_club_song
local current_queue_item

local current_boombox_sound
local boombox_player

local preloaded_next_song = false

local loudnesses = {}


function fadeSong(sound)
	fading = true
	shifting = true
	local basevol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,20 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - basevol / 20
			wait(.14)
		end
	end
	if sound then
		sound:Destroy()
	end
	shifting = false
	fading = false
end

local firsttime
if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_IntroGUI") then
	firsttime = true
end

--Shift the volume of the sound according to what the current highest loudness is
--(If starting is true, volume can be shifted up)

function ShiftVolume(sound, starting)
	if sound == nil or shifting == true or sound.SoundId == defaultid then return end
	shifting = true
	local target_volume
	
	if highest_loudness <= 200 then
		target_volume = .9
	elseif highest_loudness <= 300 then
		target_volume = .8 + (300 - highest_loudness) / 1000
	elseif highest_loudness <= 350 then
		target_volume = .7 + (350 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 400 then
		target_volume = .6 + (400 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 450 then
		target_volume = .5 + (450 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 500 then
		target_volume = .5
	elseif highest_loudness <= 675 then
		target_volume = .15 + (675 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness > 675 then
		target_volume = .1
	end
	
	if starting ~= true and sound.Volume <= target_volume then shifting = false return end
	
	local vol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,7 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - ((vol - target_volume) / 7)
			wait(1/32)
		end
	end
	
	if target_volume == .1 then --too loud
		if sound and sound.Name == "ClubMusic" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagSong:FireServer()
		elseif sound and sound.Name == "BGM" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagBoomboxSong:FireServer(boombox_player)
		end
	end
	
	shifting = false
end

function createBackgroundMusic(id,isboombox,musicset,segment)
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM") then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") and not fading then
			fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
		end
		current_club_song = nil
		if firsttime and not isboombox then
			--preload the BGM upon first joining game
			local s = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
			s.SoundId = id
			s.Volume = .001
			s:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s,.1)
			wait(4)
			firsttime = false
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.SoundId = id
		sound.Volume = (isboombox and 0) or 10 / 7
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic
		sound.Name = "BGM"
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		local i = 0
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) or i == 90 do
			wait(1/30)
			i = i + 1
		end
		if isboombox and isboombox.TimePosition > 3.5 then
			sound.TimePosition = isboombox.TimePosition
		end
		if isboombox then
			current_boombox_sound = sound
			highest_loudness = 0
			avg_loudness = 0
			loudnesses = {}
		else
			current_boombox_sound = nil
		end
		sound:Play()
		if not isboombox then
			--preload the next song
			local nextsongid
			local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
			if day.Value >= 6 and t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
				musicset = 4
				segment = 1
			else
				segment = segment + 1
				if segment > 4 then
					segment = 1
					musicset = musicset + 1
					if musicset > 4 then
						musicset = 1
					end
				end
			end
			nextsongid = songs[musicset][segment]
			if nextsongid then
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsongid
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
			end
		end
		if isboombox and vol.Value == 0 then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Your Background Music volume is set to 0%, so you will not hear the Boombox.")
		end
		if isboombox then
			shifting = true
			for i = 1,10 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
					wait(1/32)
				end
			end
			shifting = false
			
			ShiftVolume(sound, true)
		end
	end
end


-- Create Club Red DJ music, getting the ID from the queue 

function createClubMusic() 
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
		current_boombox_sound = nil
		loudnesses = {}
		avg_loudness = 0
		highest_loudness = 0
		
		local queueitem
		local nextsong
		preloaded_next_song = false
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
			if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
				queueitem = v
			elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
				nextsong = v 
			end
		end
		
		local starttime
		if queueitem then
			current_queue_item = queueitem
			starttime = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetTimeOfSong:InvokeServer()
			if starttime < 1.5 then starttime = 0 end
		end
		
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "ClubMusic"
		if queueitem then
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..queueitem.SongID.Value
		else
			sound.SoundId = defaultid
		end
		sound.Volume = 0
		sound.TimePosition = starttime
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.ClubMusic
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		current_club_song = sound
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) do
			wait(1/30)
		end
		if not sound then return end
		sound:Play()
		
		if nextsong then
			-- Preload the next song 
			local temp = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent)
			temp.Volume = 0.001
			temp.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
			temp:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(temp, .1)
			preloaded_next_song = true
		end
		
		shifting = true
		for i = 1,10 do
			if sound then
				sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
				wait(1/32)
			end
		end
		shifting = false
		
		ShiftVolume(sound, true)
	end
end



function getBackgroundMusic() --get BG music based on time/day
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local wp = weekdayspassed.Value
	
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if t >= 1380 then --get next day's music (late night)
		day = day + 1
		if day == 8 then
			day = 1
		end
	end
	
	local musicset
	if day == 1 or day == 7 then
		musicset = 4
	else
		musicset = (weekdayspassed.Value - 1) % 3 + 1
	end
	
	local segment
	if t >= 1380 or t < 300 then
		segment = 1
	elseif t >= 300 and t < 660 then
		segment = 2
	elseif t >= 660 and t < 1050 then
		segment = 3
	elseif t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
		segment = 4
	end
	
	local id = "rbxassetid://"..songs[musicset][segment]
	return id,musicset,segment
end

function isInClub(pos)
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"Club Red") then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function isInSchool(pos) --and during class hours
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"School") and day > 1 and day < 7 and t >= 420 and t < 900 then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10

vol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10
end)

clubvol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.ClubMusic.Volume = clubvol.Value / 10

-- Constantly check for club sound volume

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while true do
		wait(1/12)
		local song = current_club_song or current_boombox_sound
		if (song and song.PlaybackLoudness >= -1) then
			table.insert(loudnesses, song.PlaybackLoudness)
			if #loudnesses > 10 then
				table.remove(loudnesses, 1)
			end
			local avg = 0
			for _,v in pairs(loudnesses) do
				avg = avg + v 
			end
			avg = avg / #loudnesses
			avg_loudness = avg 
			--print("avg_loudness: ".. avg_loudness .." - highest_loudness: ".. highest_loudness .. "num loudnesses: ".. #loudnesses)
			if #loudnesses >= 8 and highest_loudness < avg then
				--print("highest loudness exceeded, shifting")
				highest_loudness = avg 
				ShiftVolume(song)
			end
		end
	end
end))

					--no change
				elseif bgm and bgm.SoundId ~= newsong then
					fadeSong(bgm)
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
					if isboombox then
						boombox_player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(isboombox.Parent.Parent)
					end
				else
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
				end
			
			elseif location == "Club Red" then
				
				local bgm = script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM")
				if bgm then
					fadeSong(bgm)
				end
				createClubMusic()
				
			end

			-- do checks on the current song playing - whether it's time to switch, or preload new song 
			local queueitem
			local nextsong
			for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
				if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
					queueitem = v
				elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
					nextsong = v 
				end
			end
			
			if queueitem ~= current_queue_item then --song change
				current_queue_item = queueitem
				if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
					fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
				end
				createClubMusic()
			end

				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
				preloaded_next_song = true
			end
		end
		
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

if objects:FindFirstChild(game.ReplicatedStorage.OtherDevProducts.StarterPack.ItemID.Value) == nil and currenttimeobj.Value - jointime < 86400 * 7 then

	script.Parent.Parent.Visible = true
else

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	timelabel.Text = GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 86400 then
		timelabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.85,0,0)
		timelabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)

		local f = Instance.new("Folder",plr)
		f.Name = "StarterPackButtonClicked"
	end
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui:Fire("StarterPackWindow")
end)

--Made by Luckymaxer

Board = script.Parent
Model = Board.Parent

MaxSpeed = 40

Players = game:GetService("Players")
ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

ActiveAnimations = {}

RemoteControl = Board:WaitForChild("RemoteControl")
Animations = Board:WaitForChild("Animations")

ServerControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ServerControl")
ClientControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ClientControl")

Animations = {
	BoardKick = Animations:WaitForChild("BoardKick"),
	CoastingPose = Animations:WaitForChild("CoastingPose"),
	LeftTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("LeftTurn"),
	RightTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("RightTurn"),
	Ollie = Animations:WaitForChild("Ollie")
}

Sounds = {
	BoardDrop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardDrop"),
	BoardOllie = Board:WaitForChild("BoardOllie"),
	BoardStop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardStop"),
	CruiseLoop = Board:WaitForChild("CruiseLoop")
}

LastAction = nil
LastTime = nil
JumpEnabled = true

SkateboardEquipped = false

--ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = (function(player, Mode, Value)
--end)

function InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	if Board.Steer == -1 then --Left turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Left")
	elseif Board.Steer == 1 then --Right turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Right")
	elseif Board.Steer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(100, 0, 100)
		Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
		JumpEnabled = true
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
end

function EnterCoastingState()
	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

function MoveStateChanged(NewState, OldState)
	if not JumpEnabled then
		EnterAirState(false)
	end
	if NewState == Enum.MoveState.AirFree then
		EnterAirState(true)
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Pushing then
		EnterPushingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function MakeOllieThrust()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	OllieThrust = Instance.new("BodyThrust")
	OllieThrust.Name = "OllieThrust"
	OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	OllieThrust.location = Vector3.new(0, 0, -2)
	OllieThrust.Parent = Board
	Stabilizer = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	Stabilizer.Name = "Stabilizer"
	Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	Stabilizer.Parent = Board
end

function MakeGyro()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	Gyro.Name = "SkateboardGyro"
	Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Gyro.P = 50
	Gyro.D = 50
	Gyro.Parent = Torso
end

function ButtonChanged(Button,Mobile)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState and not Mobile then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled and math.abs(Board.Velocity.Y) < 5 then
			JumpEnabled = false
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RIghtTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
			SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.Ollie, FadeTime = 0, Weight = 1, Speed = 4})
			Board.StickyWheels = false
			Board:ApplySpecificImpulse(Vector3.new(0, 45, 0))
			OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 1200, 0)
			DidAction("Jump")
			Delay(0.1, function()
				if OllieThrust then
					OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end)
		else
			Board.StickyWheels = true
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(humanoid, controller)
	Controller = controller
	Humanoid = humanoid
	Character = Humanoid.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	PlayerGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({Animations, Sounds}) do
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if vv:IsA("Animation") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.AnimationId)
			elseif vv:IsA("Sound") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.SoundId)
			end
		end
	end
	
	KeyDownConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and InputObject.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = true, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
		end
	end)
	KeyUpConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End and InputObject.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = false, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
		end
	end)
	
	MobileControls = nil
	
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		MobileControls = coroutine.create(function()
			while MobileControls ~= nil do
				if Emulation.w.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = 1
				elseif Emulation.s.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = -1
				else
					Board.Throttle = 0
				end
				
				if Emulation.a.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = -1
				elseif Emulation.d.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = 1
				else
					Board.Steer = 0
				end
				wait()
			end
		end)
		coroutine.resume(MobileControls)
		
		Emulation.w.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.W})
		end)
		Emulation.a.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.A})
		end)
		Emulation.s.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.S})
		end)
		Emulation.d.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.D})
		end)
		Emulation[" "].Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Space})
			if val == true then
				ButtonChanged(Enum.Button.Jump,true)
			end
		end)
		
		--[[local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
		if Emulation then
			for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
				v.Changed:connect(function(val)
					InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
				end)
			end
		end]]
	end
	
	for i, v in  pairs(Board:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("BodyThrust") or v:IsA("BodyGyro") then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	SkateboardEquipped = true
	MakeOllieThrust()
	MakeGyro()
	if not MobileControls then
		Controller.AxisChanged:connect(AxisChanged)
	end
	Board.MoveStateChanged:connect(MoveStateChanged)
	Controller:BindButton(Enum.Button.Jump, "")
	Controller.ButtonChanged:connect(ButtonChanged)
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.CoastingPose})
	InvokeServer("Equipped", Character)
end

function Unequipped()
	Controller = nil
	Board.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	for i, v in pairs({Gyro, OllieThrust, Stabilizer}) do
		if v and v.Parent then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	MobileControls = nil
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = v})
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Sounds) do
		v:Stop()
	end
	ActiveAnimations = {}
	SkateboardEquipped = false
	InvokeServer("Unequipped")
end

Board.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

TurnAlpha = 1
TurnMax = 1
AlphaDampening = 0.5

CurrentSpeed = 0
TurnSpeed = 0

ReloadTime = 15
ReloadPercent = 100
Duration = 1.5

LastAction = nil
LastTime = nil
JumpEnabled = true

SkateboardEquipped = false

--ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = (function(player, Mode, Value)
--end)

function InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function UpdateGui()
	while ReloadPercent < 100 do
		wait(ReloadTime / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,0,0)
		ReloadPercent = math.min(100, (ReloadPercent + 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
	Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
	Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
	Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = "100%"
	Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 6)
end

function DrainGui()
	while ReloadPercent > 0 do
		wait(Duration / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
		ReloadPercent = math.max(0, (ReloadPercent - 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
end

function ShowBoostGui()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	
	local EffectGui = LightSpeedEffectGui:Clone()
	local Whirl = EffectGui.Whirl
	local Background = Whirl.Background
	local Text = Whirl.TextLabel
	
	for i, v in pairs({Whirl, Background, Text}) do
		if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
			v.TextColor3 = DisplayColor
		elseif v:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			v.ImageColor3 = DisplayColor
		end
	end
	
	Debris:AddItem(EffectGui, 5)
	EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui	
	
	if PlayerGui and PlayerGui.Parent then
		EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui
	end
	
	for i = 1, 2.5, 0.02 do
		Text.TextTransparency = (Text.TextTransparency + 0.05)
		Text.TextStrokeTransparency = Text.TextTransparency
		Whirl.Size = UDim2.new(i, 1, i, 1)
		Whirl.Position = UDim2.new((0.5 - i / 2), 0, (0.5 - i / 2), 0)
		wait(0.04)
	end
	
	if EffectGui and EffectGui.Parent then
		EffectGui:Destroy()
	end
end

function GetAllConnectedParts(Object)
	local Parts = {}
	local function GetConnectedParts(Object)
		for i, v in pairs(Object:GetConnectedParts()) do
			local Ignore = false
			for ii, vv in pairs(Parts) do
				if v == vv then
					Ignore = true
				end
			end
			if not Ignore then
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				GetConnectedParts(v)
			end
		end
	end
	GetConnectedParts(Object)
	return Parts
end

function ThrustUpdater()
	while SkateboardEquipped do
		RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, TurnSpeed, 0)
		
		local Direction = Board.CFrame.lookVector
		Direction = Vector3.new(Direction.X, 0, Direction.Z).unit
		ThrustForce.velocity = (Direction * CurrentSpeed)

		if math.abs(TurnSpeed) > AlphaDampening then
			TurnSpeed = (TurnSpeed - (AlphaDampening * (math.abs(TurnSpeed) / TurnSpeed)))
		else 
			TurnSpeed = 0
		end
		local LeanAmount = (-TurnSpeed * (math.pi / 6) / 0.8)
		TurnGyro.cframe = CFrame.Angles((LeanAmount * Direction.X), 0, (LeanAmount * Direction.Z))
		
		--Compare CurrentSpeed (speed supposed to be traveling at) to actual velocity.
		local BoardVelocity = Vector3.new(Board.Velocity.X, 0, Board.Velocity.Z).magnitude
		if CurrentSpeed > 5 and (BoardVelocity + 6) < CurrentSpeed then
			CurrentSpeed = math.max((CurrentSpeed - 7),0)
		end
		
		wait(WaitTime)
	end
end

function BoostSpeed(Speed)
	EnterPushingState()
	MaxSpeed = (MaxSpeed + Speed)
	CurrentSpeed = MaxSpeed
	Spawn(function()
		ShowBoostGui()
	end)
	DrainGui()
	MaxSpeed = BaseMaxSpeed
	UpdateGui()
	JumpEnabled = true
end


function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	print("AxisChanged:",Axis)
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	CurrentSteer = Board.Steer
	
	if CurrentSteer == -1 then --Left turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Left")
		while Board.Steer == -1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 1 then --Right turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Right")
		while Board.Steer == 1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function ThrottleChanged(axis)
	print("ThrottleChanged:",axis)
	if Controller.Throttle ~= CurrentThrottle then
		CurrentThrottle = Controller.Throttle
		if CurrentThrottle > 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle > 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
		if CurrentThrottle < 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle < 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		JumpEnabled = true
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
end

function EnterCoastingState()
	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

function MoveStateChanged(NewState, OldState)
	if not JumpEnabled then
		EnterAirState(false)
	end
	if NewState == Enum.MoveState.AirFree then
		EnterAirState(true)
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Pushing then
--		EnterPushingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function SetUpControl()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	RotationForce = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
	RotationForce.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
	RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	RotationForce.Parent = Torso

	ThrustForce = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(30000, 0, 30000)
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	ThrustForce.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	ThrustForce.P = 800
	ThrustForce.Parent = Board
	
	TurnGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	TurnGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	TurnGyro.Parent = Torso
	
	local Mass = 0
	for i, v in pairs(GetAllConnectedParts(Board)) do
		Mass = (Mass + v:GetMass() * EqualizingForce * Gravity)
	end
	
	AntiGrav = Instance.new("BodyForce")
	AntiGrav.force = Vector3.new(0, (Mass / 2), 0)
	AntiGrav.Parent = Board
	
end

function ButtonChanged(Button)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled and ReloadPercent == 100 then
			JumpEnabled = false
			BoostSpeed(20)
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / TopSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Head and Head.Parent and Torso and Torso.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(humanoid, controller)
	Controller = controller
	Humanoid = humanoid
	Character = Humanoid.Parent
	Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	PlayerGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Camera = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
	Camera.CameraSubject = Head
	Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({Animations, Sounds}) do
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if vv:IsA("Animation") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.AnimationId)
			elseif vv:IsA("Sound") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.SoundId)
			end
		end
	end
	KeyDownConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = true, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
			print("true",InputObject.KeyCode)
		end
	end)
	KeyUpConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = false, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
			print("false",InputObject.KeyCode)
		end
	end)
	
	MobileControls = nil	
	
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		
		MobileControls = coroutine.create(function()
			while MobileControls ~= nil do
				if Emulation.w.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = 1
					CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (0.03))))
				elseif Emulation.s.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = -1
					CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (0.03))))
				else
					Board.Throttle = 0
				end
				
				if Emulation.a.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = -1
					TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
				elseif Emulation.d.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = 1
					TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
				else
					Board.Steer = 0
				end
				wait()
			end
		end)
		coroutine.resume(MobileControls)
		
		for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
			v.Changed:connect(function(val)
				local enum
				if v.Name == " " then
					enum = Enum.KeyCode.Space
				elseif v.Name ~= "MouseIsDown" then
					enum = Enum.KeyCode[string.upper(v.Name)]
				end
				if enum then
					InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = enum})
					if enum == Enum.KeyCode.Space and val == true then
						ButtonChanged(Enum.Button.Jump,true)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	for i, v in  pairs(Board:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("BodyForce") or v:IsA("BodyGyro") or v:IsA("BodyVelocity") or v:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	SkateboardEquipped = true
	Board.Friction = 0
	Board.StickyWheels = true
	SetUpControl()
	Spawn(function()
		if PlayerGui then
			Gui = SpeedBoostGui:Clone()
			UpdateGui()
			Gui.Parent = PlayerGui
		end
	end)
	Spawn(ThrustUpdater)
	Controller.AxisChanged:connect(AxisChanged)
	if not MobileControls then
		Controller.AxisChanged:connect(ThrottleChanged)
	end
	Board.MoveStateChanged:connect(MoveStateChanged)
	Controller:BindButton(Enum.Button.Jump, "")
	Controller.ButtonChanged:connect(ButtonChanged)
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.CoastingPose})
	InvokeServer("Equipped", Character)
end

function Unequipped()
	Controller = nil
	Board.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.Friction = 0.5
	Board.StickyWheels = false
	if CheckIfAlive() then
		Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		Camera.CameraSubject = Humanoid
		Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
	end
	for i, v in pairs({RotationForce, ThrustForce, TurnGyro, AntiGrav, Gui}) do
		if v and v.Parent then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	MobileControls = nil
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = v})
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Sounds) do
		v:Stop()
	end
	ActiveAnimations = {}
	SkateboardEquipped = false
	InvokeServer("Unequipped")
end

Board.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Board.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

RunService.Heartbeat:connect(function()
	CruiseModify()
end)k

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function GetAllConnectedParts(Object)
	local Parts = {}
	local function GetConnectedParts(Object)
		for i, v in pairs(Object:GetConnectedParts()) do
			local Ignore = false
			for ii, vv in pairs(Parts) do
				if v == vv then
					Ignore = true
				end
			end
			if not Ignore then
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				GetConnectedParts(v)
			end
		end
	end
	GetConnectedParts(Object)
	return Parts
end

function ThrustUpdater()
	while SkateboardEquipped do
		RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, TurnSpeed, 0)
		
		local Direction = Board.CFrame.lookVector
		Direction = Vector3.new(Direction.X, 0, Direction.Z).unit
		ThrustForce.velocity = (Direction * CurrentSpeed)

		if math.abs(TurnSpeed) > AlphaDampening then
			TurnSpeed = (TurnSpeed - (AlphaDampening * (math.abs(TurnSpeed) / TurnSpeed)))
		else 
			TurnSpeed = 0
		end
		
		--Compare CurrentSpeed (speed supposed to be traveling at) to actual velocity.
		local BoardVelocity = Vector3.new(Board.Velocity.X, 0, Board.Velocity.Z).magnitude
		if CurrentSpeed > 5 and (BoardVelocity + 6) < CurrentSpeed then
			CurrentSpeed = math.max((CurrentSpeed - 7), 0)
		end
		
		wait(WaitTime)
	end
end

function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	CurrentSteer = Board.Steer
	
	if CurrentSteer == -1 then --Left turn
		DidAction("Left")
		while Board.Steer == -1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 1 then --Right turn
		DidAction("Right")
		while Board.Steer == 1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function ThrottleChanged(axis)
	if Controller.Throttle ~= CurrentThrottle then
		CurrentThrottle = Controller.Throttle
		if CurrentThrottle > 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle > 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		elseif CurrentThrottle < 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle < 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		elseif CurrentThrottle == 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle == 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = (CurrentSpeed * 0.85)
				if math.abs(CurrentSpeed) <= 2 then
					CurrentSpeed = 0
				end
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
end

	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function ButtonChanged(Button)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled then
			EnterAirState(true)
			OllieThrust = Instance.new("BodyThrust")
			OllieThrust.Name = "OllieThrust"
			OllieThrust.location = Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
			local Mass = 0
			for i, v in pairs(GetAllConnectedParts(Board)) do
				Mass = (Mass + v:GetMass())
			end
			Mass = (Mass * EqualizingForce * 2)
			OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, Mass, 0)
			OllieThrust.Parent = Board
			DidAction("Jump")
			wait(0.1)
			if OllieThrust and OllieThrust.Parent then
				OllieThrust:Destroy()
			end
			EnterCoastingState()
			wait(2)
			JumpEnabled = true
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	local TopSpeed = 70
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / TopSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Head and Head.Parent and Torso and Torso.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(humanoid, controller)
	Controller = controller
	Humanoid = humanoid
	Character = Humanoid.Parent
	Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Camera = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
	Camera.CameraSubject = Head
	Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({Animations, Sounds}) do
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if vv:IsA("Animation") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.AnimationId)
			elseif vv:IsA("Sound") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.SoundId)
			end
		end
	end
	KeyDownConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = true, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
		end
	end)
	KeyUpConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = false, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
		end
	end)
	
	MobileControls = nil
	
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		MobileControls = coroutine.create(function()
			while MobileControls ~= nil do
				if Emulation.w.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = 1
				elseif Emulation.s.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = -1
				else
					Board.Throttle = 0
				end
				
				if Emulation.a.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = -1
				elseif Emulation.d.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = 1
				else
					Board.Steer = 0
				end
				wait()
			end
		end)
		coroutine.resume(MobileControls)
		
		Emulation.w.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.W})
		end)
		Emulation.a.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.A})
		end)
		Emulation.s.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.S})
		end)
		Emulation.d.Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.D})
		end)
		Emulation[" "].Changed:connect(function(val)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Space})
			if val == true then
				ButtonChanged(Enum.Button.Jump,true)
			end
		end)
		
		--[[local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
		if Emulation then
			for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
				v.Changed:connect(function(val)
					InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
				end)
			end
		end]]
	end
	
	for i, v in  pairs(Board:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("BodyForce") or v:IsA("BodyVelocity") or v:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	SkateboardEquipped = true
	Board.Friction = 0
	Board.StickyWheels = true
	SetUpControl()
	Spawn(ThrustUpdater)
	if not MobileControls then
		Controller.AxisChanged:connect(AxisChanged)
	end
	Controller.AxisChanged:connect(ThrottleChanged)
	Board.MoveStateChanged:connect(MoveStateChanged)
	Controller:BindButton(Enum.Button.Jump, "")
	Controller.ButtonChanged:connect(ButtonChanged)
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.Stand})
	InvokeServer("Equipped", Character)
end

function Unequipped()
	Controller = nil
	Board.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.Friction = 0.5
	Board.StickyWheels = false
	if CheckIfAlive() then
		Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		Camera.CameraSubject = Humanoid
		Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
	end
	for i, v in pairs({RotationForce, ThrustForce, AntiGrav}) do
		if v and v.Parent then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	MobileControls = nil
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = v})
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Sounds) do
		v:Stop()
	end
	ActiveAnimations = {}
	SkateboardEquipped = false
	InvokeServer("Unequipped")
end

Board.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

	CruiseModify()

local selectedButtonTable = {}
local oldButton = nil
function onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	if oldButton ~= nil then
		local notSelected = true
		local selectionText = ""
		for key, value in pairs(selectedButtonTable) do
			if oldButton == value then
				notSelected = false
			else
				selectionText = value.BackgroundColor.Name
			end
		end
		if notSelected then
			hoverSelection.Text = selectionText
			oldButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
		end
	end
	oldButton = nil
end

function onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton)
	onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	hoverSelection.Text = guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name
	if guiButton ~= selectedButton then
		guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
		oldButton = guiButton
	end
end

function onMouseUp(colorHolder, paletteFrame, guiButton)
	if selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] ~= nil then
		selectedButtonTable[colorHolder].Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	end
	guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Yellow()
	colorHolder.BackgroundColor = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionBox.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionLasso.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	Tool.Value.Value = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] = guiButton
	
	onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame)
	
	print("Selected Color: " .. guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name)
end

	paletteFrame.Visible = true
end

function canSelectCanvas(part)

	end

function Paint(canvas,position)

		--primaryColor.BackgroundColor

	end
end

local mouseDown = false
function on3dButton1Down(mouse) 
	mouseDown = true
	while mouseDown do
		local canvas = mouse.Target
		local pos = mouse.Hit.p
		if canvas and canvas.Name == "!Canvas" then
			if lastposition == nil or (lastposition - pos).magnitude > .033 then
				Paint(canvas,pos)
			end
		else
			mouseDown = false
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
	
end

function on3dButton1Up(mouse) 
	mouseDown = false
	lastposition = nil
end

	if not(inGui) and not(inPalette) then
		--[[mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\FillCursor.png"
		local part = mouse.Target

		if mouseDown then
			setColor(part)
		end]]
	end
end

Tool = script.Parent
Handle = Tool:WaitForChild("Handle")

Players = game:GetService("Players")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

Animations = {}

ServerControl = Tool:WaitForChild("ServerControl")
ClientControl = Tool:WaitForChild("ClientControl")

Rate = (1 / 60)

ToolEquipped = false

function SetAnimation(mode, value)
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" and value and ToolEquipped and Humanoid then
		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(value.Animation)
		table.insert(Animations, {Animation = value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(value.FadeTime, value.Weight, value.Speed)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Character and Character.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Player and Player.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(Mouse)
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	ToolEquipped = true
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	PlayerMouse = Player:GetMouse()
end

function Unequipped()
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		if v and v.AnimationTrack then
			v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
		end
	end
	Animations = {}
	ToolEquipped = false
end

function InvokeServer(mode, value)
	local ServerReturn
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(mode, value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function OnClientInvoke(mode, value)
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" and value and ToolEquipped and Humanoid then
		SetAnimation("PlayAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		SetAnimation("StopAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "PlaySound" and value then
		value:Play()
	elseif mode == "StopSound" and value then
		value:Stop()
	elseif mode == "MousePosition" then
		return ((PlayerMouse and {Position = PlayerMouse.Hit.p, Target = PlayerMouse.Target}) or nil)
	end
end

ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = OnClientInvoke
Tool.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

		if Emulation then
			oldmouse = Mouse
			for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
				v.Changed:connect(function(val)
					if ToolEquipped and v.Name == "MouseIsDown" and oldmouse == Mouse then
						InvokeServer("MouseClick", {Down = val})
						if val == true then
							repeat
								InvokeServer("MouseMove", {Position = Mouse.Hit.p, Target = Mouse.Target})
								wait(1/30)
							until v.Value == false
						end
					end
					--InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
				end)
			end
		end
		
	end
end

function Unequipped()
	ToolEquipped = false
	LocalObjects = {}
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction("Remove")
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		if v and v.AnimationTrack then
			v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({PreventJump, ObjectLocalTransparencyModifier}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	Animations = {}
end

function InvokeServer(mode, value)
	pcall(function()
		local ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(mode, value)
		return ServerReturn
	end)
end

function OnClientInvoke(mode, value)
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" and value and ToolEquipped and Humanoid then
		SetAnimation("PlayAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		SetAnimation("StopAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "PlaySound" and value then
		value:Play()
	elseif mode == "StopSound" and value then
		value:Stop()
	elseif mode == "MousePosition" then
		return {Position = PlayerMouse.Hit.p, Target = PlayerMouse.Target}
	elseif mode == "DisableJump" then
		DisableJump(value)
	elseif mode == "SetCamera" then
		pcall(function()
			Camera[value.Property] = value.Value
		end)
	elseif mode == "GetCamera" then
		local PropertyReturned = false
		local PropertyValue = nil
		pcall(function()
			PropertyValue = Camera[value.Property]
			PropertyReturned = true
		end)
		return ((PropertyReturned and PropertyValue) or nil)
	elseif mode == "SetLocalTransparencyModifier" and value and ToolEquipped then
		pcall(function()
			local ObjectFound = false
			for i, v in pairs(LocalObjects) do
				if v == value then
					ObjectFound = true
				end
			end
			if not ObjectFound then
				table.insert(LocalObjects, value)
				if ObjectLocalTransparencyModifier then
					ObjectLocalTransparencyModifier:disconnect()
				end
				ObjectLocalTransparencyModifier = RunService.RenderStepped:connect(function()
					for i, v in pairs(LocalObjects) do
						if v.Object and v.Object.Parent then
							local CurrentTransparency = v.Object.LocalTransparencyModifier
							if ((not v.AutoUpdate and (CurrentTransparency == 1 or  CurrentTransparency == 0)) or v.AutoUpdate) then
								v.Object.LocalTransparencyModifier = v.Transparency
							end
						else
							table.remove(LocalObjects, i)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = OnClientInvoke
Tool.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then

				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(value.Animation)
		table.insert(Animations, {Animation = value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(value.FadeTime, value.Weight, value.Speed)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
	end

	if PreventJump then
		PreventJump:disconnect()
	end
	if Boolean then
		PreventJump = Humanoid.Changed:connect(function(Property)
			if Property ==  "Jump" then
				Humanoid.Jump = false
			end
		end)
	end

end

	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	ToolEquipped = true
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	PlayerMouse = Player:GetMouse()
	Mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		InvokeServer("MouseClick", {Down = true})
	end)
	Mouse.Button1Up:connect(function()
		InvokeServer("MouseClick", {Down = false})
	end)
	Mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(Key)
		InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = Key, Down = true})
	end)
	Mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(Key)
		InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = Key, Down = false})
	end)
	Mouse.Move:connect(function()
		InvokeServer("MouseMove", {Position = Mouse.Hit.p, Target = Mouse.Target})
	end)
	
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
			v.Changed:connect(function(val)
				InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
			end)
		end

	Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.None)
end

		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall) --Prevent the ability to fly by constantly equipping and unequipping the tool.
	Animations = {}
end

--Made by Luckymaxer

Tool = script.Parent
Handle = Tool:WaitForChild("Handle")

Players = game:GetService("Players")
Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

RbxUtility = LoadLibrary("RbxUtility")
Create = RbxUtility.Create

BaseUrl = "http://wwww.roblox.com/asset/?id="

Animations = {
	Flying = {Animation = Tool:WaitForChild("Flying"), FadeTime = nil, Weight = nil, Speed = 0.5, Manual = true},
}

AnimationTracks = {}

Gravity = 196.20
JumpHeightPercentage = 0.25

Gliding = false
SpaceDown = false

LastTeleport = 0

BaseSpeed = 16
IncreasedSpeed = (BaseSpeed * 1.5)
FlightSpeed = (BaseSpeed * 3)

ToolEquipped = false

function CheckTableForString(Table, String)
	for i, v in pairs(Table) do
		if string.find(string.lower(String), string.lower(v)) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function CheckIntangible(Hit)
	local ProjectileNames = {"Water", "Arrow", "Projectile", "Effect", "Rail", "Laser", "Bullet"}
	if Hit and Hit.Parent then
		if ((not Hit.CanCollide or CheckTableForString(ProjectileNames, Hit.Name)) and not Hit.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function CastRay(StartPos, Vec, Length, Ignore, DelayIfHit)
	local Ignore = ((type(Ignore) == "table" and Ignore) or {Ignore})
	local RayHit, RayPos, RayNormal = game:GetService("Workspace"):FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(StartPos, Vec * Length), Ignore)
	if RayHit and CheckIntangible(RayHit) then
		if DelayIfHit then
			wait()
		end
		RayHit, RayPos, RayNormal = CastRay((RayPos + (Vec * 0.01)), Vec, (Length - ((StartPos - RayPos).magnitude)), Ignore, DelayIfHit)
	end
	return RayHit, RayPos, RayNormal
end

function SetAnimation(mode, value)
	if not ToolEquipped or not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	local function StopAnimation(Animation)
		for i, v in pairs(AnimationTracks) do
			if v.Animation == Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(value.EndFadeTime)
				if v.TrackStopped then
					v.TrackStopped:disconnect()
				end
				table.remove(AnimationTracks, i)
			end
		end
	end
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" then
		for i, v in pairs(AnimationTracks) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				if value.Speed then
					v.AnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(value.Speed)
					return
				elseif value.Weight or value.FadeTime then
					v.AnimationTrack:AdjustWeight(value.Weight, value.FadeTime)
					return
				else
					StopAnimation(value.Animation, false)
				end
			end
		end
		local AnimationMonitor = Create("Model"){}
		local TrackEnded = Create("StringValue"){Name = "Ended"}
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(value.Animation)
		local TrackStopped
		if not value.Manual then
			TrackStopped = AnimationTrack.Stopped:connect(function()
				if TrackStopped then
					TrackStopped:disconnect()
				end
				StopAnimation(value.Animation, true)
				TrackEnded.Parent = AnimationMonitor
			end)
		end
		table.insert(AnimationTracks, {Animation = value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack, TrackStopped = TrackStopped})
		AnimationTrack:Play(value.FadeTime, value.Weight, value.Speed)
		if TrackStopped then
			AnimationMonitor:WaitForChild(TrackEnded.Name)
		end
		return TrackEnded.Name
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		StopAnimation(value.Animation)
	end
end

function GetAllConnectedParts(Object)
	local Parts = {}
	local function GetConnectedParts(Object)
		for i, v in pairs(Object:GetConnectedParts()) do
			local Ignore = false
			for ii, vv in pairs(Parts) do
				if v == vv then
					Ignore = true
				end
			end
			if not Ignore then
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				GetConnectedParts(v)
			end
		end
	end
	GetConnectedParts(Object)
	return Parts
end

function SetGravityEffect(Table)
	local Table = (Table or {})
	local Multiplier = (Table.Multiplier or 1)
	if not GravityEffect or not GravityEffect.Parent then
		GravityEffect = Create(Table.Class or "BodyForce"){
			Name = "GravityCoilEffect",
		}
		for i, v in pairs(Table.Properties or {}) do
			pcall(function()
				GravityEffect[i] = v
			end)
		end
	end
	local TotalMass = 0
	local ConnectedParts = GetAllConnectedParts(Torso)
	for i, v in pairs(ConnectedParts) do
		if v:IsA("BasePart") then
			TotalMass = (TotalMass + v:GetMass())
		end
	end
	local TotalMass = ((TotalMass * Gravity) * Multiplier)
	if GravityEffect:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
		GravityEffect.maxForce = Vector3.new(0, TotalMass, 0)
	elseif GravityEffect:IsA("BodyForce") then
		GravityEffect.force = Vector3.new(0, TotalMass, 0)
	end
	GravityEffect.Parent = Torso
	return GravityEffect
end

function HandleGravityEffect(Enabled, Table)
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Torso:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("BodyForce") or v:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
			Debris:AddItem(v, 0)
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({ToolUnequipped1, DescendantAdded, DescendantRemoving}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	if Enabled then
		CurrentlyEquipped = true
		ToolUnequipped1 = Tool.Unequipped:connect(function()
			CurrentlyEquipped = false
		end)
		SetGravityEffect(Table)
		DescendantAdded = Character.DescendantAdded:connect(function()
			if not CurrentlyEquipped or not CheckIfAlive() then
				return
			end
			SetGravityEffect(Table)
		end)
		DescendantRemoving = Character.DescendantRemoving:connect(function()
			if not CurrentlyEquipped or not CheckIfAlive() then
				return
			end
			SetGravityEffect(Table)
		end)
	end
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Character and Character.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Torso and Torso.Parent and Player and Player.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Activated()
	if not ToolEquipped or not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	local Time = tick()
	local Camera = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
	if (Time - LastTeleport) > 2 then
		local Dir = Camera.CoordinateFrame.p - Torso.CFrame.p
		Dir = Vector3.new(Dir.X, 0, Dir.Z).unit
		local BodyPosition = Create("BodyPosition"){
			maxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge),
			position = (Torso.CFrame.p - Dir * 40),
		}
		Debris:AddItem(BodyPosition, 0.5)
		BodyPosition.Parent = Torso
		LastTeleport = Time
	end
end

function IsFreeFalling()
	return (CheckIfAlive() and Humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
end

function KeyPressed(Key, Down)
	if Key == " " then
		SpaceDown = Down
		local CurrentState = Humanoid:GetState().Name
		local RayHit, RayPos, RayNormal = CastRay(Torso.Position, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), (Torso.Size.Y * 2 + 0.2), {Character}, false)
		if SpaceDown and not Gliding and RayHit and CurrentState ~= "None" and CurrentState ~= "Freefall" and CurrentState ~= "FallingDown" and CurrentState ~= "GettingUp" then
			if BodyForce and BodyForce.Parent then
				Debris:AddItem(GravityEffect, 0)
			end
			BodyForce = SetGravityEffect({Multiplier = 3.5})
			Debris:AddItem(BodyForce, 0.2)
		elseif SpaceDown and not Gliding and IsFreeFalling() then
			Gliding = true
			HandleGravityEffect(true, {Class = "BodyVelocity", Properties = {P = 500, velocity = Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)}})
			for i, v in pairs({BodyForce, BodyGyro}) do
				if v and v.Parent then
					Debris:AddItem(v, 0)
				end
			end
			Torso.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			Torso.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			Humanoid.WalkSpeed = FlightSpeed
			Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			BodyGyro = Create("BodyGyro"){
				maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge),
				P = (10 ^ 5),
				Parent = Torso,
			}
			SetAnimation("PlayAnimation", Animations.Flying)
			while IsFreeFalling() and SpaceDown and Gliding and ToolEquipped do
				local Camera = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
				local Velocity = Torso.Velocity
				Velocity = Vector3.new(Velocity.X, 0, Velocity.Z)
				local FlightDirection = Camera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector
				if Velocity.Magnitude > 10 then
					FlightDirection = Velocity.Unit
				end
				FlightDirection = (FlightDirection * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1))
				local FlightCFrame = (CFrame.new(Torso.Position, (Torso.CFrame + FlightDirection * 2).p) * CFrame.Angles(-(math.pi / 2), (math.pi * 2), 0))
				if tostring(FlightCFrame.p.X) ~= "-1.#IND" and FlightCFrame.p.Y ~= "-1.#IND" and FlightCFrame.p.Z ~= "-1.#IND" then
					BodyGyro.cframe = FlightCFrame
				end
				wait()
			end
			SetAnimation("StopAnimation", Animations.Flying)
			Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
			Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
			if CheckIfAlive() then
				Humanoid.WalkSpeed = ((ToolEquipped and IncreasedSpeed) or BaseSpeed)
			end
			Gliding = false
			HandleGravityEffect(false)
			Debris:AddItem(BodyGyro, 0)
		end
	end
end

function Equipped(Mouse)
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Spawn(function()
		Humanoid.WalkSpeed = IncreasedSpeed
		Mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(Key)
			KeyPressed(Key, true)
		end)
		Mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(Key)
			KeyPressed(Key, false)
		end)
		local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
		if Emulation then
			for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
				v.Changed:connect(function(val)
					KeyPressed(v.Name,val)
				end)
			end
		end
		for i, v in pairs({HumanoidDied}) do
			if v then
				v:disconnect()
			end
		end
		HumanoidDied = Humanoid.Died:connect(function()
			if GravityEffect and GravityEffect.Parent then
				Debris:AddItem(GravityEffect, 0)
			end
		end)
		local CurrentlyEquipped = true
		if ToolUnequipped2 then
			ToolUnequipped2:disconnect()
		end
		ToolUnequipped2 = Tool.Unequipped:connect(function()
			CurrentlyEquipped = false
		end)
	end)
	ToolEquipped = true
end

function Unequipped()
	SpaceDown = false
	if CheckIfAlive() then
		Humanoid.WalkSpeed = BaseSpeed
		Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
		Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
	end
	for i, v in pairs(AnimationTracks) do
		if v and v.AnimationTrack then
			v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({HumanoidDied, ToolUnequipped2, BodyForce, BodyGyro, ToolUnequipped1, DescendantAdded, DescendantRemoving}) do
		if tostring(v) == "Connection" then
			v:disconnect()
		elseif type(v) == "userdata" and v and v.Parent then
			Debris:AddItem(v, 0)
		end
	end
	HandleGravityEffect(false)
	ToolEquipped = false
end
